| ai_credits_exhausted_body | AI-powered features are now disabled for this organization. To re-enable them, please contact {0} to add more credits. | Les fonctionnalités basées sur l'IA sont désormais désactivées pour cette organisation. Pour les réactiver, veuillez contacter {0} afin d'ajouter des crédits supplémentaires. | Body paragraph explaining that AI features are disabled and how to re-enable them. {0} is the sales contact email address. |
| ai_credits_exhausted_cta | Contact sales | Contacter l'equipe commerciale | Call-to-action button text for the AI credits exhaustion email, linking to the sales contact. |
| ai_credits_exhausted_intro | Organization {0} has exhausted its AI credits. | L'organisation {0} a épuisé ses crédits IA. | Intro paragraph for AI credits exhaustion notification. {0} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_exhausted_subject | [{0}] AI credits exhausted for "{1}" | [{0}] Crédits IA épuisés pour "{1}" | Subject for email sent to organization admins when AI credits are exhausted. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_expired_body | These credits were unused and have been removed from the organization's balance. To purchase more credits, go to Billing. | Ces crédits n'ont pas été utilisés et ont été retirés du solde de l'organisation. Pour acheter des crédits supplémentaires, accédez à la facturation. | Body paragraph explaining the expired credits were removed and how to buy more, via the organization's Billing page. |
| ai_credits_expired_cta | Go to Billing | Accéder à la facturation | Call-to-action button text for the AI credits expiration email, linking to the organization's Billing page. |
| ai_credits_expired_intro | {0} AI credits of organization {1} have reached their expiration date. | {0} crédits IA de l'organisation {1} ont atteint leur date d'expiration. | Intro paragraph for AI credits expiration notification. {0} is the number of expired credits and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_expired_subject | [{0}] AI credits expired for "{1}" | [{0}] Crédits IA expirés pour "{1}" | Subject for email sent to organization admins when AI credits expire. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_order_status_canceled | canceled | annulée | Status of an AI credits order that was canceled, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_order_status_chargeback | charged back | rétrofacturée | Status of an AI credits order whose card payment was reclaimed by the cardholder's bank after a lost chargeback dispute, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_order_status_refund | refunded | remboursée | Status of an AI credits order that was refunded, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_order_status_reversed | reversed | contrepassée | Status of an AI credits order whose authorized payment was reversed, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_refund_deducted_detail | Order {0} was marked as {1}, so {2} credits were deducted. Current balance: {3} credits. | La commande {0} a été marquée comme {1}, {2} crédits ont donc été déduits. Solde actuel : {3} crédits. | Detail paragraph explaining the deduction. {0} is the order reference, {1} is the localized refund/cancel status the order transitioned to (see ai_credits_order_status_*), {2} is the locale-formatted credits deducted, {3} is the locale-formatted current wallet balance. |
| ai_credits_refund_deducted_intro | AI credits previously granted to organization {0} have been reversed. | Les crédits IA précédemment accordés à l'organisation {0} ont été annulés. | Intro paragraph for the AI credits deduction notification. {0} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_refund_deducted_subject | [{0}] AI credits adjusted for "{1}" | [{0}] Crédits IA ajustés pour "{1}" | Subject for email sent to organization admins when previously granted AI credits are deducted because the order was refunded/canceled after being processed as a purchase. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_topup_skipped_detail | Order {0} had already been marked as {1} before the purchase confirmation arrived, so no credits were added. Current balance: {2} credits. | La commande {0} était déjà marquée comme {1} avant l'arrivée de la confirmation d'achat, aucun crédit n'a donc été ajouté. Solde actuel : {2} crédits. | Detail paragraph explaining why the top-up was skipped. {0} is the order reference, {1} is the localized refund/cancel status the order already had (see ai_credits_order_status_*), {2} is the locale-formatted current wallet balance. |
| ai_credits_topup_skipped_intro | An AI credits purchase for organization {0} was not applied. | Un achat de crédits IA pour l'organisation {0} n'a pas été appliqué. | Intro paragraph for the skipped AI credits top-up notification. {0} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_topup_skipped_subject | [{0}] AI credits top-up skipped for "{1}" | [{0}] Recharge de crédits IA ignorée pour "{1}" | Subject for email sent to organization admins when an AI credits purchase's top-up is skipped because the order was already refunded/canceled. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |

| authentication.social.sign.up.info | Users who sign up with Google or GitHub do not receive a role automatically. An organization administrator must assign a role to grant appropriate access. {line_break}{line_break} If a social provider is later disabled, users who originally signed up with it can select "Forgot my password" to set a password and continue signing in with their email address. | Les utilisateurs qui s’inscrivent avec Google ou GitHub ne reçoivent pas automatiquement de rôle. Un administrateur de l’organisation doit leur attribuer un rôle pour leur accorder les droits d’accès appropriés. {line_break}{line_break} Si un fournisseur social est ensuite désactivé, les utilisateurs qui s’y sont initialement inscrits peuvent sélectionner « Mot de passe oublié » pour définir un mot de passe et continuer à se connecter avec leur adresse e-mail. | Title for users without a platform role |
| authentication.sso.note | Users can join this organization without an invitation by signing up with a configured single sign-on provider. | Les utilisateurs peuvent rejoindre cette organisation sans invitation en s'inscrivant via un fournisseur d'authentification unique configuré. |  |

| project.access.request.again.at | You can request access again at {time}. | Vous pourrez demander l'accès à nouveau à {time}. | Tells when the user may request access to a project again. {time} is a time of day, e.g. 11:42 AM. |

| project.access.request.description | You don't have access to this project. Only project members can open its files, branches and publications. | Vous n'avez pas accès à ce projet. Seuls les membres du projet peuvent ouvrir ses fichiers, ses branches et ses publications. | Description on the page shown to a user who opens a project they are not a member of. Must not name the project. |
| project.access.request.hint | Request access and the project owners will be notified by email. | Demandez l'accès et les propriétaires du projet seront avertis par e-mail. | Explains what requesting access to a project does. |
| project.access.request.message.label | Message to owners (optional) | Message aux propriétaires (facultatif) | Label of the optional message a user sends to the owners of a project when requesting access to it. |
| project.access.request.message.placeholder | Tell the owners why you need access | Expliquez aux propriétaires pourquoi vous avez besoin d'accès | Placeholder of the optional message a user sends to the owners of a project when requesting access to it. |
| project.access.request.pending.sent.on | Request pending · sent {date} | Demande en attente · envoyée le {date} | Status of a request for access to a project sent before today. {date} is a short date, e.g. Sep 24, 2026. |
| project.access.request.pending.sent.today | Request pending · sent today at {time} | Demande en attente · envoyée aujourd'hui à {time} | Status of a request for access to a project sent today. {time} is a time of day, e.g. 10:42 AM. |
| project.access.request.title | Request access to this project | Demander l'accès à ce projet | Title of the page shown to a user who opens a project they are not a member of. |
| project.access.requested.description | Your request was sent to the project owners. They'll choose your project role. | Votre demande a été envoyée aux propriétaires du projet. Ils choisiront votre rôle dans le projet. | Shown right after the user asked the owners of a project for access to it. |
| project.access.requested.returning.description | You already requested access to this project. The project owners haven't responded yet. | Vous avez déjà demandé l'accès à ce projet. Les propriétaires du projet n'ont pas encore répondu. | Shown when the user comes back to a project whose owners they already asked for access, within the hour. |
| project.access.requested.title | Access requested | Accès demandé | Title of the page shown once the user asked the owners of a project for access to it. |
| project.access.requested.your.message | Your message | Votre message | Label of the message the user sent with their request for access to a project. |

| project.type.dita.tooltip | DITA is for maps, topics, and related DITA files. With this type, you can organize publications, publish to PDF and WebHelp, review DITA quality in Problems, reuse content with keys and components, run translation jobs, search within the current map, and insert images and media from the Content library. | DITA convient aux maps, topics et fichiers DITA associés. Avec ce type, vous pouvez organiser des publications, publier en PDF et WebHelp, contrôler la qualité DITA dans Problèmes, réutiliser du contenu avec des keys et des composants, lancer des travaux de traduction, rechercher dans la map courante et insérer des images et des médias depuis la bibliothèque de contenu. | Shown on the DITA option when choosing project type, and on the type in settings. |
| project.type.generic | Generic | Générique | Project type option for non-DITA content. DITA is the acronym and is not translated. |
| project.type.generic.tooltip | Generic is for projects that are not DITA. With this type, you can store any mix of files, including other XML formats such as DocBook or S1000D, Markdown, and non-XML documents. You can edit files, create review tasks, and search across the project. DITA-specific features are not available, including publications, PDF and WebHelp publishing, DITA quality checks in Problems, content reuse with keys and components, translation jobs, search within the current map, and inserting images and media from the Content library. | Générique convient aux projets qui ne sont pas DITA. Avec ce type, vous pouvez stocker n'importe quel mélange de fichiers, y compris d'autres formats XML tels que DocBook ou S1000D, du Markdown et des documents non XML. Vous pouvez modifier des fichiers, créer des tâches de révision et rechercher dans le projet. Les fonctionnalités spécifiques à DITA ne sont pas disponibles, notamment les publications, la publication PDF et WebHelp, les contrôles de qualité DITA dans Problèmes, la réutilisation de contenu avec keys et composants, les travaux de traduction, la recherche dans la map courante et l'insertion d'images et de médias depuis la bibliothèque de contenu. | Shown on the Generic option when choosing project type, and on the type in settings. |
| project.type.immutable.note | Project type can't be changed. | Ne peut pas être modifié ultérieurement. | Shown under the project type selector and on settings. Type cannot be changed after creation. |
| project.url | Project URL | URL du projet | A project's URL input label. |

| project_member_role_updated_body_to_collaborator | {0} changed your role to collaborator on the project: | {0} a changé votre rôle en collaborateur dans le projet : | Body text for email sent when a user's role was changed to COLLABORATOR in a project. {0} is the acting user's name and email. |
| project_member_role_updated_body_to_maintainer | {0} changed your role to maintainer on the project: | {0} a changé votre rôle en mainteneur dans le projet : | Body text for email sent when a user's role was changed to MAINTAINER in a project. {0} is the acting user's name and email. |
| project_member_role_updated_body_to_owner | {0} changed your role to owner on the project: | {0} a changé votre rôle en propriétaire dans le projet : | Body text for email sent when a user's role was changed to OWNER in a project. {0} is the acting user's name and email. |

| read_only_because_task_closed | The file is read-only because the task's owner has closed the review. | Le fichier est en lecture seule car le propriétaire de la tâche a clôturé la révision. | Task is not active. Files in task are read-only and can be open read-only |

| read_only_because_task_is_integrating | This file is read-only because it belongs to a task that is being integrated by an owner. | Ce fichier est en lecture seule car il appartient à une tâche en cours d'intégration par un propriétaire. | Task is being integrated by an owner. Files in task are read-only for the other users (both Reviewers and Owners). |

| role_change_info_author | As an Author, you now have access to the Projects functionality, including the ability to create and manage projects. | En tant qu'Auteur, vous avez maintenant accès à la fonctionnalité Projets, y compris la possibilité de créer et gérer des projets. | Info for users who become AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_contributor | As a Contributor, you can be assigned as a collaborator on projects to suggest changes to their files by creating review tasks from your suggestions. | En tant que Contributeur, vous pouvez être assigné comme collaborateur sur des projets pour proposer des modifications à leurs fichiers en créant des tâches de révision à partir de vos suggestions. | Info for users who become CONTRIBUTOR, coming from a role other than AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_contributor_from_author | As a Contributor, your access to the Projects functionality is now limited to that of a project collaborator: you can be assigned as a collaborator on projects to suggest changes to their files by creating review tasks from your suggestions. | En tant que Contributeur, votre accès à la fonctionnalité Projets est désormais limité à celui d'un collaborateur de projet : vous pouvez être assigné comme collaborateur sur des projets pour proposer des modifications à leurs fichiers en créant des tâches de révision à partir de vos suggestions. | Info for users who become CONTRIBUTOR after previously being AUTHOR: unlike SME, they keep some project access, just limited to what a project collaborator has. |
| role_change_info_none | Your platform role has been set to "No license seat". Vous conserverez l'accès administrateur aux paramètres de votre organisation, mais ne consommerez pas de siège de licence payant. | Info for users who are demoted to NONE (no platform role, no license seat consumed). |
| role_change_info_sme | As a Subject Matter Expert, you can create and manage review tasks. | En tant qu'Expert en la matière, vous pouvez créer et gérer des tâches de révision. | Info for users who become SME, coming from a role other than AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_sme_from_author | As a Subject Matter Expert, you no longer have access to the Projects functionality. | En tant qu'Expert en la matière, vous n'avez plus accès à la fonctionnalité Projets. | Info for users who become SME after previously being AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_sme_from_contributor | As a Subject Matter Expert, you still have access to review tasks, but no longer have access to the projects you were a collaborator on. | En tant qu'Expert en la matière, vous avez toujours accès aux tâches de révision, mais vous n'avez plus accès aux projets sur lesquels vous étiez collaborateur. | Info for users who become SME after previously being CONTRIBUTOR: they keep working with review tasks, but lose the project-collaborator access they had (SME has no project access at all). |

| save_new_external_resources_message | The task contains modified documents that were originally stored on a remote server, as well as new documents.\n The modified documents are opened in separate editor tabs. You need to manually save them (according to the protocol required by the remote server).\n The newly added documents must be saved locally (click "Choose directory" to save them) and then manually uploaded on the remote server. | La tâche contient des documents qui ont été stockés à l'origine sur un serveur distant, ainsi que de nouveaux documents.\n Les documents modifiés sont ouverts dans des onglets d'édition séparés. Vous devez les sauvegarder manuellement (selon le protocole requis par le serveur distant).\n Les documents nouvellement ajoutés doivent être sauvegardés localement (cliquez sur "Choisir un répertoire" pour les sauvegarder) puis téléversés manuellement sur le serveur distant. | Message presented to the user when there are new external resources are applied. |
